回顧與展望                                     REVIEW AND PROSPECTS

2025年，中國房地產市場在政策持續調控下，                    In 2025, amidst ongoing policy adjustments and regulations, the
正逐步朝著穩定健康的方向發展。2025年二季                    China’s real estate market is steadily moving towards stable and healthy
度以來政策面推進速度加快，房地產金融環境                      development. Since the second quarter of 2025, policy implementation
持續寬鬆，各地政府積極落實中央穩市場的要                      has been accelerated. Under a relaxing financial environment for the
求，專項債回購土地公告發佈頻次提升，多地                      real estate industry, many local governments have, in response to the
出台促消費政策，並進一步放開行政管控以促                      requirements of the central government for market stabilisation, frequently
進剛需和改善性需求釋放。                              issued announcements on acquisition of land through special bonds,
                                          introduced various policies to promote consumption, and further eased
                                          administrative regulations in order to further release of the demand for
                                          both rigid and improved housing.

發揮優勢，展現出良好的經營韌性，在變化中                      further leverages its strengths to demonstrate robust operational
尋求新機遇，於挑戰中開拓新局面。                          resilience, seeking new opportunities amidst changes and creating new

本集團開發並持有若干商業物業，以賺取租金               The Group develops and holds several commercial properties such as
收入或取得資本增值，例如購物廣場及停車位               shopping malls and car parks for rental income or capital appreciation.
等。本集團的投資物業每半年由獨立物業估值               The Group’s investment properties are appraised semi-annually by an
師進行評估。本集團投資物業的升值或貶值部               independent property valuer. Any appreciation or depreciation in the
分於本集團簡明合併損益表中確認為公允價值               Group’s investment property value is recognised as fair value gains or
收益或虧損。截至2025年6月30日止六個月，            losses in the Group’s condensed consolidated statement of profit or

由於受房地產行業環境的影響，估值略有下降。              30 June 2025 were approximately RMB83.5 million, which was mainly

半年度利潤及使用非香港財務報告準                        Half-year Profit and use of non-HKFRS measures
則計量

司 權 益 持 有 人 的 淨 利 潤 約 人 民 幣 691.6 百 萬   approximately RMB691.6 million for the half year ended 30 June 2025,
元，同比截至2024年6月30日止半年度下降                  representing a year-on-year decrease of 27.9% compared to the half
27.9% ， 變 動 主 要 受 營 業 額 、 銷 售 及 服 務 成   year ended 30 June 2024, the change was mainly due to the combined
本、銷售及營銷成本、行政開支、融資成本淨                    effect of factors such as revenue, sales and service costs, selling and
額、所得稅開支等的綜合影響。                          marketing costs, administrative expenses, net financing costs, and
                                        income tax expenses.

為補充根據香港財務報告準則編製及呈列的合                    To supplement the consolidated financial statements prepared and
併財務報表，我們採用非香港財務報告準則經                    presented in accordance with HKFRS, we utilise non-HKFRS adjusted
調整核心盈利作為額外財務計量。核心盈利為                    core earnings as an additional financial measure. Core earnings is a non-
一項非香港財務報告準則計量，有助評估本集                    HKFRS measure for facilitating the evaluation of financial performance of
團核心業務營運的財務表現，其主要排除以下                    the Group’s core operations, which primarily excludes the impact of (i) fair
各項的影響：(i)投資物業及金融資產公允價值                  value losses on investment properties and financial assets of RMB76.2
虧損人民幣76.2百萬元；(ii)與借款及金融資產               million; (ii) unrealised foreign exchange gains relating to borrowings and
有關的未變現外匯收益人民幣10.7百萬元及(iii)              financial assets of RMB10.7 million; and (iii) losses on acquisition and
收購處置子公司等的損失人民幣8.6百萬元。                   disposal of subsidiaries of RMB8.6 million.

董事會認為，排除上述屬非經常性質且未能反                    The Board believe that the exclusion of the above items which are
映本集團於報告期間的經營表現的項目，將有                    non-recurring in nature and not indicative of the Group’s operating
助於本公司股東（「股東」）及潛在投資者評估本                  performance during the Reporting Period would facilitate the evaluation
集團核心業務的財務表現。                            of financial performance of the Group’s core operations by shareholders
                                        of the Company (the “Shareholders”) and potential investors of the
                                        Company.

然而，使用非香港財務報告準則計量作為分析                    However, the use of non-HKFRS measures has limitations as an analytical
工具有局限性，故不應將其與根據香港財務報                    tool, and should not consider it in isolation from, or as substitute for
告準則所報告的經營業績或財務狀況的分析分                    analysis of, our results of operations or financial conditions as reported
開考慮或予以取代。此外，其他公司所用的類                    under HKFRSs. In addition, these non-HKFRS financial measures may be
似詞彙可能會對該非香港財務報告準則計量有                    defined differently from similar terms used by other companies, and may
不同定義，因此可能無法與其他公司使用的類                    not be comparable to other similarly measures used by other companies.
似計量進行比較。

股份獎勵計劃                       SHARE AWARD SCHEME

於2019年11月1日，董事會決議採納股份獎勵      On 1 November 2019, the Board has resolved to adopt a share award
計劃（「股份獎勵計劃」）。股份獎勵計劃的宗旨       scheme (the “Share Award Scheme”). The objectives of the Share
為：(i)肯定本公司僱員（「僱員」）所作出的貢獻     Award Scheme are: (i) to recognize the contribution of employees of the
及獎勵僱員對本集團未來的長期發展繼續作出         Company (the “Employees”) and to provide incentives for the Employees
重大貢獻；及(ii)吸引及挽留可能對本集團成長      to continuously make substantial contributions for the Group’s long-term
及發展有益的優秀僱員。                  growth in the future; and (ii) to attract and retain talented Employees who
                             may be beneficial to the growth and development of the Group.

 董事會就管理股份獎勵計劃而委任的受託人         The aggregated maximum number of shares of the Company (the
（「受託人」）   （為獨立第三方）可購買的最高本公   “Shares”) that the trustee (who is an independent third party) appointed

 2019年11月1日全部已發行股本的0.5%，即    purchase must not exceed 0.5% of the total share capital in issue of the
 29,495,000股股份（佔本報告日期本公司已發   Company on 1 November 2019, i.e. 29,495,000 shares (representing
 行股份約0.42%）。                 approximately 0.42% of the issued shares of the Company as at the date
                             of this report).

10月31日止十年。於本報告日期，股份獎勵計       up to 31 October 2029. As at the date of this report, the remaining life of
劃的剩餘年期約為四年又兩個月。              the Share Award Scheme was around four years and two months.

                    的公允價值按報告期末的市                              quoted market prices at the end of the reporting period.
                    場報價釐定。本集團所持有                              The quoted market price used for financial assets held

                    當時買盤價。該等工具計入                              are included in level 1.
                    第一級。

                    賣的金融工具（如受限制上市                             not traded in an active market (such as restricted listed
                    股 本 證 券 等 ）的 公 允 價 值 採                    equity securities etc.) is determined using valuation
                    用估值法釐定，該等估值法                              techniques which maximise the use of observable
                    盡量採用可觀察市場數據，                              market data and rely as little as possible on entity-
                    並盡可能不倚賴實體特定估                              specific estimates. If all significant inputs required to fair
                    計。倘計算工具公允價值需                              value an instrument are observable, the instrument is
                    要的所有重要輸入值均為可                              included in level 2.
                    觀察數據，則該工具計入第
                    二級。

                    第三級：倘一項或多項重要                              Level 3: If one or more of the significant inputs is not

                    數據，則該工具計入第三                               included in level 3. This is the case mainly for unlisted

    5.4 資本風險管理                                          5.4 Capital risk management

         本集團的資本管理目標為保障本集團                                  The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard
         能繼續營運，以為股東提供回報和維                                  the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order to
         持最佳的資本結構以降低資金成本。                                  provide returns for shareholders and to maintain an optimal
                                                           capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.

         為維持或調整資本結構，本集團可能                                  In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group
         會調整支付予股東的股息金額、發行                                  may adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders, issue
         新股份或出售資產以減少債務。                                    new shares or sell assets to reduce debt.

         本集團利用資產負債比率監察其資                                   The Group monitors capital on the basis of the gearing ratio.
         本。此比率按照債務淨額除以資本總                                  This ratio is calculated as net debt divided by total capital. Net
         額計算。債務淨額按借款總額及租賃                                  debt is calculated as total borrowings plus lease liabilities less
         負債減現金及現金等價物計算。資本                                  cash and cash equivalents. Total capital is calculated as total
         總額按合併財務狀況表所列示的權益                                  equity, as shown in the consolidated statement of financial
         總額加上債務淨額計算。                                       position, plus net debt.

    等物業各自按現況交吉出售。經參考有關                      approach. Direct comparison approach is assuming sale of each
    市場的可資比較銷售交易後，已選擇鄰近                      of these properties in its existing state with the benefit of vacant
    地區的可資比較物業並就位置及物業規模                      possession. By making reference to sales transactions as available
    等因素的差異作出調整。                             in the relevant market, comparable properties in close proximity have
                                            been selected and adjustments have been made to account for the
                                            difference in factors such as locations and property size.

    項目將產生的成本以反映已竣工開發項目                      costs incurred and the costs that will be incurred to complete the
    的質量，依據是該等物業將根據本集團最                      development to reflect the quality of the completed development
